- **bmad-review**: Runs one or more installed review lenses — adversarial critique, edge cases, verification gaps, structure, prose — and reports triaged findings. Use when, and only when, the user asks you to review a diff, a pull request, or an artifact — code or documents, one or many — and actually says "review"; an explicit skill:bmad-review directive from another skill counts as that ask. A request to act on feedback from an earlier review is a change, not a review. Never invoke this uninvited, including on edits you just made. - **bond-challenge**: Fetch the wallet-ownership challenge for a provider bond (#1967). Prints the nonce and canonical message to sign with the provider's registered wallet; pass the resulting signature via --wallet on ``bond create``/``bond top-up`` (or sign manually and post challenge_nonce/wallet_signature to the API). ([oib/AITBC](https://github.com/oib/AITBC/blob/HEAD/docs/cli/commands/bond-challenge.md))
- **bond-create**: Create or top-up a provider's performance bond. Non-admin callers must prove control of the provider's registered wallet: pass --wallet/--private-key/--private-key-file and the CLI fetches a challenge and signs it automatically (#1967). Without a key source the request goes unsigned — admin JWTs still pass, provider callers get 403. ([oib/AITBC](https://github.com/oib/AITBC/blob/HEAD/docs/cli/commands/bond-create.md))
- **bond-lock**: Lock a provider's bond while a high-value job is in flight. ([oib/AITBC](https://github.com/oib/AITBC/blob/HEAD/docs/cli/commands/bond-lock.md))
- **bond-lock-onchain**: Submit the on-chain BOND_LOCK escrow transaction for a provider bond. ``bond create``/``top-up`` record the bond coordinator-side but require a prior chain-verified ``BOND_LOCK``; this command is that missing leg. The tx pays ``--escrow-address`` (the node's ``BOND_ESCROW_ADDRESS``) with payload ``{bond_id, provider, lock_days}`` where ``provider`` is the signing wallet's own address — the chain r ([oib/AITBC](https://github.com/oib/AITBC/blob/HEAD/docs/cli/commands/bond-lock-onchain.md))
- **bond-release**: Release a previously locked provider bond. The coordinator's release endpoint un-locks a LOCKED bond back to ACTIVE: the bond record and its amount stay posted — release is not a withdrawal, and there is no endpoint that frees the bonded funds. ``status: "active"`` in the response therefore *is* the released state, not a failure. ([oib/AITBC](https://github.com/oib/AITBC/blob/HEAD/docs/cli/commands/bond-release.md))

- **bos-frame**: Phase 0 — turn an idea or complaint into a stated problem, a named ICP and a falsifiable riskiest assumption ([mariomile/builder-os](https://github.com/mariomile/builder-os/blob/HEAD/commands/bos-frame.md) · ★2)
- **bos-gate**: Run the current phase's gate check and report pass/fail per condition ([mariomile/builder-os](https://github.com/mariomile/builder-os/blob/HEAD/commands/bos-gate.md) · ★2)
- **bos-ideate**: Phase 3 — generate distinct solution options, select the bet, write kill criteria, and design the cheapest test ([mariomile/builder-os](https://github.com/mariomile/builder-os/blob/HEAD/commands/bos-ideate.md) · ★2)
- **bos-init**: Start a BuilderOS project or a new initiative in one: PRODUCT.md, DOMAIN.md, TECH.md, the roadmap and the .builderos/ state directory ([mariomile/builder-os](https://github.com/mariomile/builder-os/blob/HEAD/commands/bos-init.md) · ★2)
- **bos-learn**: Add a memory manually to the BOS self-evolving memory system. ([opensesh/karimo-overview](https://github.com/opensesh/karimo-overview/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/bos-learn.md) · ★11)
- **bos-learn**: Phase 7 — measure the shipped change against its target and its kill criteria, decide keep, iterate or kill, and record the learning ([mariomile/builder-os](https://github.com/mariomile/builder-os/blob/HEAD/commands/bos-learn.md) · ★2)
- **bos-refresh**: Regenerate the L0/L1 context summaries in `.bos/context/`. ([opensesh/karimo-overview](https://github.com/opensesh/karimo-overview/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/bos-refresh.md) · ★11)
- **bos-review**: Product review: read every lever of the metric tree, judge the stage, propose at most three initiatives for the person to pick, and update the roadmap ([mariomile/builder-os](https://github.com/mariomile/builder-os/blob/HEAD/commands/bos-review.md) · ★2)
- **bos-search**: Search the BOS context cache using semantic similarity. ([opensesh/karimo-overview](https://github.com/opensesh/karimo-overview/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/bos-search.md) · ★11)
- **bos-shape**: Phase 4 — turn the selected bet into a spec: flows, business rules, the look and the stack decided with the user, bounded scope, testable acceptance criteria, full state coverage, and the tracking plan ([mariomile/builder-os](https://github.com/mariomile/builder-os/blob/HEAD/commands/bos-shape.md) · ★2)
- **bos-ship**: Phase 6 — rollout plan, tested rollback, production instrumentation check, baseline captured before exposure, release notes, scheduled review ([mariomile/builder-os](https://github.com/mariomile/builder-os/blob/HEAD/commands/bos-ship.md) · ★2)
- **bos-status**: One-screen view of the BuilderOS project: every initiative, the active one's phases and gates, what blocks the next step ([mariomile/builder-os](https://github.com/mariomile/builder-os/blob/HEAD/commands/bos-status.md) · ★2)
- **bos-sync-karimo**: Sync KARIMO learnings and PRD artifacts to the BOS context and memory systems. ([opensesh/karimo-overview](https://github.com/opensesh/karimo-overview/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/bos-sync-karimo.md) · ★11)
- **boson-datapath-audit**: Show what boson built for the arithmetic on the critical paths — chosen adder architecture, depth on each path, and which logic rewrites restructured it. ([partcleda/boson-explore-plugin](https://github.com/partcleda/boson-explore-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/boson-datapath-audit.md))
- **boson-dc-datapath-compare**: Split the QoR gap between a boson netlist and a Design Compiler netlist of the same RTL into the part inside the datapath operators and the part outside them, and compare isolated operators. ([partcleda/boson-explore-plugin](https://github.com/partcleda/boson-explore-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/boson-dc-datapath-compare.md))
- **boson-diagnose**: Work out why a boson result is bad (decisions on the failing paths, datapath architecture, a deep construct, or the gap to a reference netlist) and, if wanted, package anonymized evidence for partcl. ([partcleda/boson-explore-plugin](https://github.com/partcleda/boson-explore-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/boson-diagnose.md))
- **boson-dse**: Sweep clock period and RTL parameters through boson and report the area/power/timing tradeoff. ([partcleda/boson-explore-plugin](https://github.com/partcleda/boson-explore-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/boson-dse.md))
- **boson-help**: Find or understand a boson command, flag, app variable or error through boson's own help, before assuming a gap. ([partcleda/boson-explore-plugin](https://github.com/partcleda/boson-explore-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/boson-help.md))
- **boson-liberty-shapes**: Check that boson reads every table shape in your Liberty files — 3-D constraints, unusual axis orders, 1-D/scalar tables, index overrides — by loading one real example of each shape and timing it at exact table points. ([partcleda/boson-explore-plugin](https://github.com/partcleda/boson-explore-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/boson-liberty-shapes.md))
- **boson-minimal-repro**: Find out why a few paths are far deeper than the clock allows — rebuild the construct as a module-level boson run, A/B equivalent rewrites of it, and write a debug report. ([partcleda/boson-explore-plugin](https://github.com/partcleda/boson-explore-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/boson-minimal-repro.md))
- **boson-netlist-debug**: Work out what boson got wrong in a run of your design (bad netlist decisions + current issues) and build an anonymized, leak-checked debug bundle to send partcl. ([partcleda/boson-explore-plugin](https://github.com/partcleda/boson-explore-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/boson-netlist-debug.md))
- **boson-ppa-optimize**: Close timing at a target clock with the cheapest boson recipe that works (effort → draws → candidate race → datapath search → ECO), and/or find fmax. ([partcleda/boson-explore-plugin](https://github.com/partcleda/boson-explore-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/boson-ppa-optimize.md))
- **boson-reference-compare**: Explain why a boson netlist is worse than a reference netlist of the same RTL and library (another tool's, or an earlier boson version's) by mapping pattern, buffer tree and failing-path family, and write ranked findings partcl can act on. ([partcleda/boson-explore-plugin](https://github.com/partcleda/boson-explore-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/boson-reference-compare.md))
- **boson-rtl-evolve**: Evolve the RTL against a PPA objective (Fmax, area or power, with optional caps) with boson as the evaluator. Every candidate passes an equivalence gate before it is scored. ([partcleda/boson-explore-plugin](https://github.com/partcleda/boson-explore-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/boson-rtl-evolve.md))
- **boson-rtl-fix**: Edit the RTL until boson meets timing — probe critical paths, make one reviewable change at a time, re-probe, confirm at real effort. ([partcleda/boson-explore-plugin](https://github.com/partcleda/boson-explore-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/boson-rtl-fix.md))
- **boson-sweep-video**: Run a boson design-space sweep and render it as an animated PPA video/GIF (terminal replay + Pareto scatter + placements). ([partcleda/boson-explore-plugin](https://github.com/partcleda/boson-explore-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/boson-sweep-video.md))

- **bossdream-implement**: Ejecuta el plan.md de la feature activa INCREMENT POR INCREMENT. Por cada increment implementa, corre el pipeline del increment, y PARA a pedir permiso con un informe de cambios justificado. NO commitea (los commits son del usuario). NO auto-invocable: solo lo corre el usuario, y correrlo ES su autorizacion explicita para implementar. TRIGGER — el usuario lo ejecuta tras /bossdream-plan. ([MiguelALRendon/BossDream](https://github.com/MiguelALRendon/BossDream/blob/HEAD/plugins/bossdream-core/commands/bossdream-implement.md) · ★2)
- **bossdream-init**: Materializa la capa de proyecto de BossDream (docs/, plans/, .claude, pipeline.json, completion.json, CI) en el repo actual, de forma idempotente y reutilizando lo que ya exista. Al terminar indica al usuario que archivos debe rellenar y donde. ([MiguelALRendon/BossDream](https://github.com/MiguelALRendon/BossDream/blob/HEAD/plugins/bossdream-core/commands/bossdream-init.md) · ★2)
- **bossdream-learning-review**: Corre el gate de 4 etapas de bossdream-learning (evidence_gate/pattern_consistency_gate deterministas + validacion/aprobacion humana) sobre un patron detectado, y si pasa, ofrece publicarlo como tutorial procedural. TRIGGER — el usuario quiere revisar si una operacion repetida (ej. registrar TC-XXX) ya califica para publicarse como tutorial reusable. ([MiguelALRendon/BossDream](https://github.com/MiguelALRendon/BossDream/blob/HEAD/plugins/bossdream-core/commands/bossdream-learning-review.md) · ★2)
- **bossdream-mode**: Declara explicitamente el tipo de la tarea actual (el modo). Modos NO-editables (plan, document, review) bloquean toda edicion de codigo. Modos editables sin spec (spike, explore, bugfix, chore) permiten editar rutas de dominio sin frenar para arreglos ad-hoc. `feature` (o sin modo) exige spec en rutas de dominio. `clear` borra el modo. Para EJECUTAR un plan usa `/bossdream-implement`, no este comando. ([MiguelALRendon/BossDream](https://github.com/MiguelALRendon/BossDream/blob/HEAD/plugins/bossdream-core/commands/bossdream-mode.md) · ★2)
- **bossdream-new**: Crea un proyecto NUEVO desde cero con gobernanza BossDream desde el dia uno (greenfield). Toma la idea del proyecto (inline) o un archivo de requerimientos como semilla, planea, guia el scaffold del esqueleto (receta de stack-pack o herramienta nativa), y cablea la gobernanza. Para instalar BossDream sobre un proyecto que YA existe, usa /bossdream-init. TRIGGER — el usuario quiere arrancar un proyecto nuevo. ([MiguelALRendon/BossDream](https://github.com/MiguelALRendon/BossDream/blob/HEAD/plugins/bossdream-core/commands/bossdream-new.md) · ★2)
- **bossdream-override**: Llave de emergencia - destraba UNA invocacion de /bossdream-plan o /bossdream-implement bloqueada por validation-lock.ps1, sin desactivar el gate para el resto del proyecto. TRIGGER - el usuario necesita avanzar de urgencia (ej. fix de produccion) con validation.json.overallStatus en blocked y no puede esperar a resolver los hallazgos de Document Validation. ([MiguelALRendon/BossDream](https://github.com/MiguelALRendon/BossDream/blob/HEAD/plugins/bossdream-core/commands/bossdream-override.md) · ★2)
- **bossdream-plan**: De una historia cruda del usuario a un plan ejecutable por increments. Investiga (codigo + web), verifica impacto cross-flow, propone 2-3 opciones y construye el plan INCREMENTALMENTE: cada increment pasa su revision de diseno antes de escribirse al MD. Escala la ceremonia al tamano (trivial-&gt;directo, task-&gt;ligero, grande-&gt;deep-plan). TRIGGER — el usuario describe QUE quiere lograr (una historia/necesidad), no una solucion ya hecha. NOTA: se llama bossdream-plan (NO plan) para no colisionar con el Plan Mode NATIVO de Claude Code. ([MiguelALRendon/BossDream](https://github.com/MiguelALRendon/BossDream/blob/HEAD/plugins/bossdream-core/commands/bossdream-plan.md) · ★2)
- **bossdream-project**: Arranca un proyecto de negocio nuevo con gobernanza de documentos SDLC. Desde una idea o documentos de entrada, clasifica Simple/Medio/Complejo, propone Epics/Features cuando aplique, genera requirements.md (incluida su seccion de Reglas de negocio) via docs-rewrite reusado, y deja tests/test-approach.md crudo, con placeholders y el aviso de validacion estricta ya integrado en la plantilla. TRIGGER — el usuario quiere iniciar la documentacion de negocio de una iniciativa nueva, antes de poder correr /bossdream-validate o /bossdream-plan sobre ella. ([MiguelALRendon/BossDream](https://github.com/MiguelALRendon/BossDream/blob/HEAD/plugins/bossdream-core/commands/bossdream-project.md) · ★2)
- **bossdream-qa-mode**: Fija el modo de QA persistente del proyecto (`bossdream.json &gt; qa.mode`) que usan `qa-run` y el QA Increment. Valores optimo, agresivo o costoso (default). No es `/bossdream-mode`, que declara el tipo de tarea. Sin argumento muestra el modo actual. ([MiguelALRendon/BossDream](https://github.com/MiguelALRendon/BossDream/blob/HEAD/plugins/bossdream-core/commands/bossdream-qa-mode.md) · ★2)
- **bossdream-report**: Genera overview.md/understanding.md del proyecto activo bajo demanda, en docs/reports/&lt;slug&gt;/ — fuera del alcance de validation-check.ps1. Reportes humanos legibles (que se entendio y que se va a construir), reusando las plantillas de templates/_PROJECT_TEMPLATE/ a partir de requirements.md/traceability.json/tests/test-approach.md. TRIGGER — el usuario quiere un resumen humano del proyecto activo, sin que eso agregue documentos al gate de Document Validation. ([MiguelALRendon/BossDream](https://github.com/MiguelALRendon/BossDream/blob/HEAD/plugins/bossdream-core/commands/bossdream-report.md) · ★2)
- **bossdream-set-active-project**: Activa, cambia o desactiva el proyecto activo (.claude/.active-project) — puntero LOCAL por desarrollador, nunca compartido via git. Sin argumento, informa el estado actual. TRIGGER — el usuario quiere retomar un proyecto que otro dev ya documento, cambiar de proyecto para apoyar en otro, o volver al modo normal (bossdream-plan/bossdream-implement sin gobernanza de proyecto) para una tarea suelta. ([MiguelALRendon/BossDream](https://github.com/MiguelALRendon/BossDream/blob/HEAD/plugins/bossdream-core/commands/bossdream-set-active-project.md) · ★2)
- **bossdream-skill-exploration**: Audita/actualiza el catalogo completo de skills del proyecto — mapea el repo entero, corre tech-debt a nivel de todo el proyecto, y disena con el usuario (via brainstorming) cada skill nueva o desactualizada que tech-debt confirme. TRIGGER — proceso unico y puntual: el usuario lo invoca cuando quiere auditar/actualizar el catalogo completo de skills del proyecto, sin instruccion adicional del usuario sobre que explorar. ([MiguelALRendon/BossDream](https://github.com/MiguelALRendon/BossDream/blob/HEAD/plugins/bossdream-core/commands/bossdream-skill-exploration.md) · ★2)
- **bossdream-sync**: Reconcilia los archivos gestionados por el framework (wiring de hooks en settings, plantillas de pipeline/completion, esqueletos de docs) con la version actual del plugin, sin tocar el contenido del equipo ni del usuario. ([MiguelALRendon/BossDream](https://github.com/MiguelALRendon/BossDream/blob/HEAD/plugins/bossdream-core/commands/bossdream-sync.md) · ★2)
- **bossdream-sync-models**: Sincroniza el `model:` del frontmatter de cada agente en `plugins/bossdream-core/agents/` con `bossdream.json &gt; models`. ([MiguelALRendon/BossDream](https://github.com/MiguelALRendon/BossDream/blob/HEAD/plugins/bossdream-core/commands/bossdream-sync-models.md) · ★2)
- **bossdream-testcase**: Genera un nuevo caso de prueba (TC-XXX.md) para el proyecto activo, presentado al usuario para aprobar/corregir/rechazar antes de registrarlo. Cada corrida reabre validation.json a blocked. TRIGGER — el usuario quiere agregar un caso de prueba formal (Medio/Complejo) a un proyecto ya scaffoldeado. ([MiguelALRendon/BossDream](https://github.com/MiguelALRendon/BossDream/blob/HEAD/plugins/bossdream-core/commands/bossdream-testcase.md) · ★2)
- **bossdream-update-docs**: Registra los cambios que el usuario hizo a mano en los documentos del proyecto activo (ediciones a documentos existentes, o alcance nuevo que aparecio y necesita integrarse a la jerarquia Epic/Feature/US). Reabre el Document Validation cuando corresponde. TRIGGER — el usuario edito documentos manualmente y quiere que el sistema se entere, o describe alcance nuevo que todavia no esta escrito en requirements.md. ([MiguelALRendon/BossDream](https://github.com/MiguelALRendon/BossDream/blob/HEAD/plugins/bossdream-core/commands/bossdream-update-docs.md) · ★2)
- **bossdream-validate**: Corre el pipeline de Document Validation batcheado por check (no documento por documento) sobre el proyecto activo, con la profundidad que le toque segun su clasificacion S/M/C. Repetible hasta que todos los documentos queden verified. TRIGGER — el usuario termino de revisar/ajustar los documentos generados por /bossdream-project (especialmente tests/test-approach.md) y quiere desbloquear /bossdream-plan. ([MiguelALRendon/BossDream](https://github.com/MiguelALRendon/BossDream/blob/HEAD/plugins/bossdream-core/commands/bossdream-validate.md) · ★2)
